#include <stdio.h>

int factorial(int n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * factorial(n - 1);
}

int main() {
    int n = 5;
    printf("Factorial of %d is %d\n", n, factorial(n));
    return 0;
}

class BankAccount:
    def __init__(self, owner, balance=0):
        self.owner = owner
        self.balance = balance

    def deposit(self, amount):
        if amount <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Amount must be positive")
        self.balance += amount
        return self.balance

SELECT u.id, u.name, COUNT(o.id) AS order_count
FROM users u
LEFT JOIN orders o ON u.id = o.user_id
WHERE u.age >= 18 AND u.status = 'active'
GROUP BY u.id, u.name
HAVING COUNT(o.id) > 3
ORDER BY order_count DESC
LIMIT 10;

#include <stdio.h>

int factorial(int n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * factorial(n - 1);
}

int main() {
    int n = 5;
    printf("Factorial of %d is %d\n", n, factorial(n));
    return 0;
}

SELECT u.id, u.name, COUNT(o.id) AS order_count
FROM users u
LEFT JOIN orders o ON u.id = o.user_id
WHERE u.age >= 18 AND u.status = 'active'
GROUP BY u.id, u.name
HAVING COUNT(o.id) > 3
ORDER BY order_count DESC
LIMIT 10;

class BankAccount:
    def __init__(self, owner, balance=0):
        self.owner = owner
        self.balance = balance

    def deposit(self, amount):
        if amount <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Amount must be positive")
        self.balance += amount
        return self.balance

public class Main {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        int[] arr = {5, 3, 8, 1, 9};
        int max = arr[0];
        for (int i = 1; i < arr.length; i++) {
            if (arr[i] > max) {
                max = arr[i];
            }
        }
        System.out.println("Max value: " + max);
    }
}

class BankAccount:
    def __init__(self, owner, balance=0):
        self.owner = owner
        self.balance = balance

    def deposit(self, amount):
        if amount <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Amount must be positive")
        self.balance += amount
        return self.balance

#include <stdio.h>

int factorial(int n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * factorial(n - 1);
}

int main() {
    int n = 5;
    printf("Factorial of %d is %d\n", n, factorial(n));
    return 0;
}

class BankAccount:
    def __init__(self, owner, balance=0):
        self.owner = owner
        self.balance = balance

    def deposit(self, amount):
        if amount <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Amount must be positive")
        self.balance += amount
        return self.balance

#include <stdio.h>

int factorial(int n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * factorial(n - 1);
}

int main() {
    int n = 5;
    printf("Factorial of %d is %d\n", n, factorial(n));
    return 0;
}

#include <stdio.h>

int factorial(int n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * factorial(n - 1);
}

    int n = 5;
    printf("Factorial of %d is %d\n", n, factorial(n));

    def __init__(self, owner, balance=0):
        self.owner = owner
        self.balance = balance

        if amount <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Amount must be positive")
        self.balance += amount
        return self.balance

#include <stdio.h>

int factorial(int n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * factorial(n - 1);
}

int main() {
    int n = 5;
    printf("Factorial of %d is %d\n", n, factorial(n));
    return 0;
}

class BankAccount:
    def __init__(self, owner, balance=0):
        self.owner = owner
        self.balance = balance

    def deposit(self, amount):
        if amount <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Amount must be positive")
        self.balance += amount
        return self.balance

#include <stdio.h>

int factorial(int n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * factorial(n - 1);
}

int main() {
    int n = 5;
    printf("Factorial of %d is %d\n", n, factorial(n));
    return 0;
}

SELECT u.id, u.name, COUNT(o.id) AS order_count
FROM users u
LEFT JOIN orders o ON u.id = o.user_id
WHERE u.age >= 18 AND u.status = 'active'
GROUP BY u.id, u.name
HAVING COUNT(o.id) > 3
ORDER BY order_count DESC
LIMIT 10;

int factorial(int n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * factorial(n - 1);
}

SELECT u.id, u.name, COUNT(o.id) AS order_count
FROM users u
LEFT JOIN orders o ON u.id = o.user_id
WHERE u.age >= 18 AND u.status = 'active'
GROUP BY u.id, u.name
HAVING COUNT(o.id) > 3
ORDER BY order_count DESC
LIMIT 10;

class BankAccount:
    def __init__(self, owner, balance=0):
        self.owner = owner
        self.balance = balance

    def deposit(self, amount):
        if amount <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Amount must be positive")
        self.balance += amount
        return self.balance

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        int[] arr = {5, 3, 8, 1, 9};
        int max = arr[0];
        for (int i = 1; i < arr.length; i++) {
            if (arr[i] > max) {
                max = arr[i];
            }
        }

    }
}

#include <stdio.h>

int factorial(int n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * factorial(n - 1);
}

int main() {
    int n = 5;
    printf("Factorial of %d is %d\n", n, factorial(n));
    return 0;
}

class BankAccount:
    def __init__(self, owner, balance=0):
        self.owner = owner
        self.balance = balance

    def deposit(self, amount):
        if amount <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Amount must be positive")
        self.balance += amount
        return self.balance

#include <stdio.h>

int factorial(int n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * factorial(n - 1);
}

int main() {
    int n = 5;
    printf("Factorial of %d is %d\n", n, factorial(n));
    return 0;
}

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        int[] arr = {5, 3, 8, 1, 9};
        int max = arr[0];

                max = arr[i];

        }

public class Main {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        int[] arr = {5, 3, 8, 1, 9};
        int max = arr[0];
        for (int i = 1; i < arr.length; i++) {
            if (arr[i] > max) {
                max = arr[i];
            }
        }
        System.out.println("Max value: " + max);
    }
}

SELECT u.id, u.name, COUNT(o.id) AS order_count
FROM users u
LEFT JOIN orders o ON u.id = o.user_id
WHERE u.age >= 18 AND u.status = 'active'
GROUP BY u.id, u.name
HAVING COUNT(o.id) > 3
ORDER BY order_count DESC
LIMIT 10;

    def __init__(self, owner, balance=0):
        self.owner = owner
        self.balance = balance

    def deposit(self, amount):
        if amount <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Amount must be positive")
        self.balance += amount
        return self.balance

SELECT u.id, u.name, COUNT(o.id) AS order_count
FROM users u
LEFT JOIN orders o ON u.id = o.user_id
WHERE u.age >= 18 AND u.status = 'active'
GROUP BY u.id, u.name
HAVING COUNT(o.id) > 3
ORDER BY order_count DESC
LIMIT 10;

#include <stdio.h>

int factorial(int n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * factorial(n - 1);
}

int main() {
    int n = 5;
    printf("Factorial of %d is %d\n", n, factorial(n));
    return 0;
}

public class Main {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        int[] arr = {5, 3, 8, 1, 9};
        int max = arr[0];
        for (int i = 1; i < arr.length; i++) {
            if (arr[i] > max) {
                max = arr[i];
            }
        }
        System.out.println("Max value: " + max);
    }
}
